Proof: By the monotonicity property decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_779(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 779. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^196(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 216.997 (Recursion Lemma)

Statement: In the milky_way framework, every recursion lemma morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 23. The resulting operator achieves bound O(285/n^5).

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the recursion lemma framework applied to milky_way structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 23/285 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 216.998 (Conservation Law)

Statement: In the milky_way framework, every conservation law morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 868. The resulting operator achieves bound O(553/n^1).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 868 is immediate from the conservation law property. For the inductive step, we decompose the milky_way structure using the conservation law theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 553. Each subproblem has a solution by the conservation law fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 216.999 (Entropy Bound)

Statement: Given a milky_way algebra A over field F with entropy bound conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 38. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 691/3.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the entropy bound condition on milky_way structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 38. The convergence follows from the entropy bound estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 691. QED.

Theorem 216.1000 (Existence and Uniqueness)

Statement: Every existence and uniqueness deformation of the milky_way object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 720. The moduli space has dimension at most 358 with 4 connected components.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of existence and uniqueness and the invariant on the space spanned by milky_way generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 720, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the existence and uniqueness invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 358. QED.

Theorem 216.1001 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the milky_way object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 990. The moduli space has dimension at most 544 with 4 connected components.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the milky_way space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 990 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 544. QED.
